### Page 1

M
Update
King Yuan Electronics Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
2027 outlook remains
promising; OW
4Q26 guidance cut from the major product delay and weaker smartphone
demand: The company cut 4Q26 revenue guidance  from up >10% to high-single
digit QoQ growth, given the R-series GPU shipment delays and weaker smartphone/
consumer demand. The gross margin should also decline slightly to 36-37%, given
the capacity build but lower-than-expected utilization rate.

1Q27 and 2027 demand remains robust: However, the company reiterated that
delayed demand will be pushed out to 1Q27, suggesting 1Q27 revenue will be up at
least 10-15% QoQ and gross margin will recover to >40%. Looking ahead into 2027,
demand remains robust, and the company expects 2027 revenue to grow 40-50%
YoY with margins >40%.
KYEC in-house burn-in oven could start to support MediaTek TPU: Despite  the
shipment delay from Micro Control Company (MCC), MediaTek management still

raised its 4Q26 TPU revenue projection for both 4Q26 and 2027. We believe it
relates to KYEC's supports of its in-house burn-in oven by end-2026, which could
potentially leave more share to KYEC against ASE if MCC's oven shipments continue
to fall short in  2027.

Key Takeaways
3Q26 results largely in line with both MSe and consensus, but 4Q26 both
revenue and margin guidance missed.

Despite the near-term headwind from the supply chain leading to KYEC's weaker
performance, we expect GPU, TPU, and CPU demand to all ramp up rapidly from
1Q27.
KYEC US capacity is on track for mass production by 4Q27 and full-year
contribution in 2028.
We stay OW on robust 2027 demand, margin recovery, and new capacity
expansion with strong customers' commitment.
November 5, 2026 10:10 AM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Yuan Electronics Co Ltd (2449.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity of 9.8%, intermediate-term
growth rate of 10.5%, terminal growth rate of 3.0%, and cash dividend payout ratio of 65%.
Risks to Upside
n Rise in shipments for core customers such as MediaTek
n Strong AI demand from both GPU and ASIC
n Penetration of wafer sorting business
n Rise in IDM outsourcing to OSAT

Risks to Downside
n Overall non-AI demand remaining weak
n Significant testing market share loss
n Drop in shipments for core customers such as MediaTek

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Missed on Margins
No strong near-term catalysts despite trough valuation.
3Q26  results:
• Off revenue of NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), GM declined 6.5ppt QoQ
to 52.2%, significantly below MSe at 57.6%/consensus at 58.0%. We think
this is probably due to less-favorable revenue mix.
• Operating profit of NT$2,643mn (OPM 26.6%) was 21% lower than MSe at
NT$3,330mn (OPM 33.4%)/19% off consensus at NT$3,281mn (OPM 33.6%).

• Net profit  at NT$2,652mn (EPS of NT$2.31) was 24% below MSe at NT
$3,491mn (EPS NT$3.03)/20% off consensus at NT$3,307mn (EPS NT$2.79).
October revenue was NT$3,747mn (+4% MoM/+74% YoY):
• 33% of both our 4Q26 revenue estimate at NT$11,459mn (+15% QoQ/+63%
YoY) and  consensus of NT$11,263mn (+13% QoQ/+61% YoY).
Other announcements (see more in Other announcements ):
•   E Ink announced spending of NT$2.78bn acquiring the land and factories in
Guanyin (Taiwan) from its related party Chunghwa Pulp  for its large-size

signage capacity.
• It  partners with Taiflex  for the latter to do CCL pilot production at E Ink's
facility, with potentially room for collaboration on mass production in the
future.
• It announced it would invest NT$187mn in the rights issuance from its
related party SinoPac FHC.
Our view:
• 4Q26 revenue will need to grow by 10% QoQ to put overall 2026 revenue at
10% YoY (low end of its guidance at 10-15% YoY), which implies Nov-26 and
Dec-26 could be down low single digits % MoM each. While it still looks

within reach, we suspect this is at least partially riding the tailwinds of more
module shipments, which might continue to suppress its gross margin.
• Although the  valuation is at a trough level of 11x 2027e P/E, we see no
strong catalysts in the near term except for the potential fund flows amid
the subdued AI appetite. That said, we remain a believer of longer-term e-
paper demand across various applications.
• 3Q26 earnings call is scheduled at 2:30pm HKT on November 12.

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.

3Q26 Results and Other Announcements
3Q26  results
• Off revenue of NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), gross margin declined by 6.5ppt
QoQ to 52.2%, significantly below MSe at 57.6% and consensus at 58.0%. We think
this is probably owing to a less-favorable revenue mix.
• Opex was NT$2,548mn (+4% QoQ/+16% YoY; opex ratio of 25.6%) vs. slightly
higher than MSe at NT$2,416mn (opex ratio 24.2%) and consensus at NT$2,386mn
(opex ratio 24.4%).
• Operating profit of NT$2,643mn (OPM 26.6%) was 21% lower than MSe at NT

$3,330mn (OPM 33.4%) and 19% below consensus at NT$3,281mn (OPM 33.6%).
• With smaller non-operating income of NT$406mn, net profit came in at NT
$2,652mn (EPS of NT$2.31), 24% lower than MSe at NT$3,491mn (EPS of NT$3.03)
and 20% below consensus at NT$3,307mn (EPS of NT$2.79).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus
EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.

Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Preview: Margins to Stay Elevated on Cost Pass-Through; Raise 
TP to NT$140, Stay Neutral
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI will report 3Q26 results in mid-Nov. We now expect net profit of 
NT$3.19bn and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above con estimates, as 3Q26 GPM 
stays elevated at 15.8% vs. our previous 12.0% estimates, supported by 
better VGA supply and continued ASP pass-through of rising memory 
costs. For 4Q26E, we expect MB/VGA shipments to ease QoQ but ASPs to 
rise, with server contribution to further increase. Looking into 2027E, we 
expect PC demand to remain weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases pace slows down. We raise 2026E-28E earnings by 
10-57% and lift our TP to NT$140 (from NT$118; 12x 2027E EPS). We stay 
Neutral, with likely limited upside given uncertain PC recovery and modest 
server contribution. 
3Q26 preview — MSI reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$56.9bn (+14% QoQ/-1% YoY), 
broadly in line with Citi’s forecast and slightly ahead of cons. While Sep MB and VGA 
shipments both exceeded expectations, as VGA supply improved. With memory and 
other raw material prices continuing to rise and MSI able to pass costs through, we 
expect 3Q26 GPM to stay elevated at 15.8%, well above our previous 12.0% estimate. 
Assuming only a modest increase in opex ratio, we forecast net profit of NT$3.19bn 
(+6% QoQ/+88% YoY) and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above cons forecasts.
Price hike continues — We expect 4Q26E shipments to decline from 3Q26’s peak 
season. Mgmt. also indicated that Oct shipment could go down from Sep high base. 
However, we raise our 4Q26E pricing expectations and believe ASP should stay 
elevated, as memory prices keep climbing and MSI continues to pass costs through. 
The long-cultivated server business should also contribute higher than previous 
quarters. Looking into 2027, PC demand should remain weak as component costs 
stay high; we expect MSI’s MB/VGA/PC shipments to decline by double digits, with 
pass-through room narrowing as the price hike pace slows down.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 10-57%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through. We lift our TP to NT$140 (from 
NT$118), implying 12x 2027E EPS (rolled over from 12x 2026E EPS). We believe the 
share price already reflects MSI’s ability to pass costs through to end products, and 
we see limited upside, given that the PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain 
and the contribution from the new server business is still limited. Stay Neutral.
n
Michael Hung AC

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$140 is based on 12x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.
If you are visually impaired and would like to speak to a Citi representative regarding the details

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를

저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 09 월 30일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

Document 247: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원  
현재가 (10/6) 1,310,000원

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22

Status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다.

Sell 추천기준일 직전 1 개월(20 거래일)간 평균종가대비 -20% 미만  매도 0.0% 
산업 시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중 대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천  2026년 9 월 30 일 기준으로  
최근 1 년간 금융투자상품에 대하여

투자의견 BUY

6개월 목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

Document 275: Anthropic IPO定价在即：高盛预测2030年营收破6850亿，7位创始人IPO套现17.5亿美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO定价在即：高盛预测2030年营收破6850亿，7位创始人IPO套现17.5亿美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛预测 Anthropic 营收将从 2025 年 46 亿美元增⾄ 2030 年 6850 亿美元，增⻓逾 130 倍，但
⾃由现⾦流需⾄ 2030 年⽅才转正。此次 IPO 估值约 1.4 万亿美元，七位联合创始⼈合计套现
17.5 亿美元，各⾃出售约 2.5 亿美元股份，仅占持股约 1.5% 。分析⼈⼠认为套现⽐例较
低，不⾜以动摇投资者信⼼。
摘要：
Anthropic 正以史上最具野⼼的 IPO 姿态叩响资本市场⼤⻔，⽽其主承销⾏⾼盛的预测模型为这
场盛宴提供了最强⼒的背书。
11 ⽉ 10 ⽇，据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露，⾼盛研究团队预测 Anthropic 到 2030 年营收将达约
6850 亿美元，较该公司 2025 年的 46 亿美元增⻓逾 130 倍。
与此同时华尔街⻅闻提及， Anthropic 已启动 IPO 路演，寻求以约 1.4 万亿美元的完全稀释后估值
完成融资，计划于 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇（周三）定价，次⽇开始交易。
承销银⾏周⼆上午已开始与潜在投资者举⾏会议，推介这笔⽬标融资规模达 904 亿美元（含 260

亿美元私募配售）的 IPO 。公司七位联合创始⼈将在此次发⾏中合计套现 17.5 亿美元，各⾃出售
约 2.5 亿美元股份，占各⾃持股⽐例约 1.5% 。
这份增⻓预测折射出市场对 AI 技术改造经济潜⼒的⾼度乐观，但也令投资者⾯临⼀道核⼼抉择：
当前的 AI 估值究竟反映的是可信赖的增⻓轨迹，还是⼀个尚未经过验证的投资主题的过度亢奋？
⾼盛预测路径：营收 130 倍增⻓， 2030 年⽅才转正
据彭博报道，⾼盛研究团队在向潜在投资者作⼝头简报时披露了⼀套详细的营收预测路径：
Anthropic 今年总营收预计约 520 亿美元， 2027 年升⾄ 1360 亿美元， 2029 年达到 4420 亿美元，
2030 年进⼀步冲⾼⾄ 6850 亿美元以上。
在收⼊结构上，⾼盛预计 2030 年企业客⼾收⼊将占绝⼤多数，达 5860 亿美元，⾮企业收⼊约
1000 亿美元。
值得注意的是，这份预测同样呈现出 Anthropic 短期内的财务压⼒。⾼盛判断，受⾼额资本开⽀
拖累，该公司要到 2030 年才能实现全年⾃由现⾦流转正，届时⾃由现⾦流预计为 336 亿美元。

在此之前，公司预计将于 2026 年第四季度出现 128 亿美元的⾃由现⾦流流出， 2027 年进⼀步扩
⼤⾄ 657 亿美元。
在成本端，⾼盛预计 Anthropic 算⼒基础设施⽀出将在⼗年末攀升⾄近 4000 亿美元，较当前⽔
平增⻓逾⼗倍。与此同时，算⼒端利润也将以相近幅度提升。申报⽂件显⽰， Anthropic 已承诺
在未来数年投⼊约 9000 亿美元⽤于算⼒采购。
经营亏损⾼峰在即，盈利能⼒路径清晰
Anthropic ⽬前仍是⼀家处于⾼速扩张期的亏损企业。
据英国《⾦融时报》报道，该公司 2025 年运营亏损达 81 亿美元，今年前九个⽉收窄⾄ 13 亿美
元。公司⾃ 2023 年起从未实现运营盈利。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
若剔除股票薪酬⽀出及⾮现⾦慈善捐赠， Anthropic 今年前九个⽉调整后运营利润为 41 亿美元。
⾼盛的预测模型显⽰，公司运营亏损将于 2027 年达到峰值 404 亿美元，随后逐步收窄，并于
2029 年实现运营层⾯转正， 2030 年运营利润将达 652 亿美元。
Anthropic 在周⼆更新的申报⽂件中披露，其年化营收运⾏率（ ARR ）于 10 ⽉份达到 810 亿美
元，较上⽉增加 100 亿美元。
分析认为这份预测所依托的核⼼逻辑，是 AI ⼯具对⼤范围专业活动的替代潜⼒。从 Claude Code
被软件开发者⼴泛采⽤，到未来可能覆盖新药研发、复杂法律与会计规则的⾃动化处理，乃⾄
制造业⾃动化，⽀持者相信这只是开端。
然⽽，市场分歧依然存在。投资者正在权衡的，是这些预测究竟代表⼀项将重塑经济与社会的
技术的可信增⻓轨迹，还是⼀个尚未证明持久⽣命⼒的投资主题的过度乐观。
⾼盛的模型以 " ⼝头简报 " 形式与潜在投资者沟通，⽽⾮以书⾯报告形式公开发布，这⼀细节也折
射出当前 AI 预测体系的不确定性。
创始⼈套现与市场定价逻辑
Anthropic 七位联合创始⼈将在此次 IPO 中合计套现 17.5 亿美元。按 IPO 价格区间中值计算，每
位创始⼈将出售约 2.5 亿美元股份，彭博亿万富翁指数显⽰，届时每⼈净资产约为 180 亿美元。
七位联合创始⼈分别为 Dario Amodei 、 Daniela Amodei 、 Tom Brown 、 Jack Clark 、 Jared
Kaplan 、 Sam McCandliss 和 Christopher Olah 。
创始⼈在 IPO 中⼤规模套现通常被市场视为看空信号。不过，佛罗⾥达⼤学沃灵顿商学院荣休教
授 Jay R. Ritter 认为，此次套现不太可能令潜在投资者望⽽却步。他表⽰：
他以⾕歌 2004 年 IPO 为例，当时 Larry Page 、 Sergey Brin 及时任 CEO Eric Schmidt 分别套现约
4100 万美元和 3100 万美元。
Ritter 进⼀步指出，此次套现仅占每位创始⼈持股总量的约 1.5% ，且 Dario Amodei 和 Daniela
Amodei 去年的现⾦薪酬仅约 65 万美元，绝⼤部分财富依然锁定在股权中。他说：
据 Anthropic 招股书， Dario Amodei 和 Daniela Amodei 此前均参与了 2025 年 6 ⽉及 2026 年 4 ⽉的
要约回购。

市场已经越来越能接受创始⼈和⼤股东在 IPO 时部分变现，就美元⾦额⽽⾔，这确实偏⾼，但绝
⾮没有先例。
部分⼈坐拥巨额账⾯财富，却⼏乎没有现⾦，适度变现是市场不会过分担忧的事情。

Document 276: Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 三季度营收达 100 亿美元，季环⽐增⻓近 50% ，年化运⾏速率约 500 亿美元，但运
营亏损仍从 101 亿扩⼤⾄ 109 亿美元。竞争对⼿ Anthropic 启动 IPO 路演，三季度营收 170 亿
美元，亏损仅 5.67 亿，在体量与盈利能⼒上均领先。
摘要：
OpenAI 最新财务数据显⽰，其营收增⻓势头强劲，但盈利⽬标依然遥远。
11 ⽉ 10 ⽇，据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露，OpenAI 三季度（截⾄ 9 ⽉末）营收达 100 亿美元，季
环⽐增⻓近 50% ，同⽐增幅接近三倍。
调整后运营利润率从 -150% 则从改善⾄ -109% 。尽管利润率有所提升，但绝对亏损⾦额仍⼩幅扩
⼤，从 101 亿美元升⾄ 109 亿美元。
上述财务数据于周⼆在⼀场投资者电话会议中披露。CFO Sarah Friar 将这⼀季度定性为 " 爆炸
性季度 " ，并透露公司年化营收运⾏速率已达约 500 亿美元。
竞争对⼿ Anthropic 正于同⽇启动 IPO 路演。据 Anthropic 此前披露，其第三季度营收已达 170 亿

美元，同期运营亏损仅 5.67 亿美元，⽆论在体量还是盈利能⼒上均⼤幅领先 OpenAI 。
OpenAI 营收⾼增⻓难掩亏损扩⼤，继续加码企业市场
据 Sarah Friar 介绍，三季度营收增⻓的核⼼驱动⼒来⾃前沿研究和企业业务。
她表⽰， OpenAI 企业业务单季度实现翻倍增⻓，推动企业端与消费者端收⼊⽐例趋近于五五
开。在具体产品层⾯， Friar 点名了编程⼯具 Coding 、⽹络安全产品 Cyber 以及企业 AI 智能体平台
Dots 。她说：
中⼩企业业务同样录得加速，三季度增速超过此前六个季度的总和。然⽽，运营亏损从 101 亿美
元⼩幅上升⾄ 109 亿美元，表明公司在控制成本⽅⾯仍⾯临较⼤压⼒。
OpenAI 正将更多资源押注于利润率更⾼的企业市场，以缩⼩与 Anthropic 之间的差距。Friar 坦
⾔， Anthropic 在企业端的成功为 OpenAI 提供了重要的战略参照。
她表⽰，此前公司在微软 Azure 合同框架内受到约束，限制了与第三⽅云服务商开展分发合作的

空间。 Friar 说：
为强化这⼀战略，公司近期任命 Dali Rajic 出任⾸席营收官，专职负责企业业务板块的拓展。企
业市场在 OpenAI 整体营收中的占⽐正持续提升。
正是这些产品在企业端加速落地，推动了业绩增⻓。
我认为将这⼀限制解开之后， Anthropic 向我展⽰了这种模式的强⼤⼒量，对我们⽽⾔，这在
2027 年可以成为⾮常重要的增⻓顺⻛。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
10 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
消费者端⽅⾯，Friar 披露消费者业务季环⽐销售额提升 20% ，付费订阅⽤⼾数突破 1 亿。公司整
体每周活跃⽤⼾达 12 亿。值得注意的是，与 OpenAI 不同， Anthropic 不设消费端业务，增⻓完全
依赖企业市场。
推出商业主⻚功能，剑指⾕歌本地搜索
在产品层⾯，华尔街⻅闻提及，周⼆公司宣布推出 "Businesses in ChatGPT" 测试版，允许企业
认领并管理其在 ChatGPT 上的商业主⻚，追踪⽤⼾互动数据，并通过专属 AI 代理与潜在客⼾直
接对话。
符合条件的企业还可创建专属的业务代理（ Business Agent ），使 ChatGPT ⽤⼾能够直接向其提
问，获取关于该企业及其产品的信息。
此外，商业中⼼还将向企业提供有限度的数据洞察，涵盖个⼈主⻚浏览量、品牌提及次数、⽹

站点击量等指标，这⼀设计令⼈联想到⾕歌分析（ Google Analytics ）的功能定位。
在验证机制⽅⾯，企业须经过正式流程完成⾝份核验。 OpenAI 货币化副总裁 Benji Shomair 表
⽰，公司针对不同市场、不同类型的企业采⽤多种验证⽅式，但未披露具体细节。
双雄竞速上市，估值⻆⼒进⼊关键窗⼝
融资与上市进程为两家公司的竞争增添了新的维度。
华尔街⻅闻提及， Anthropic 已于周⼆启动 IPO 路演，计划上市时间为 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇或 20 ⽇，⽬标估
值约为 1.4 万亿美元。
OpenAI 则选择将 IPO 计划推迟⾄明年，⽬前正以 1.25 万亿美元估值推进新⼀轮融资，已完成⾸
批 110 亿美元的股权募集。公司表⽰，所筹资⾦将⽤于⽀撑持续⾼强度的资本开⽀需求。
尽管两家公司在营收确认⽅式上存在差异，但三季度的业绩对⽐显⽰出明显的收敛态势 ——
Anthropic 以约 170 亿美元的绝对营收保持领先， OpenAI 则以更快的加速度弥合差距。
在各⾃ IPO 叙事成型的关键节点，这场营收竞赛的⾛势将直接影响投资者对两家公司⻓期商业潜

⼒的定价判断。

in September, although the annual pace

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth in September, while
the annual pace moved into contraction at –0.8%yr. The
result was well above both Westpac’s expectation for a
7.0%mth decline and the market expectation for a 2.0%mth
rise. Unit approvals continued to drive headline volatility,

surging in September, while detached dwellings, which
provide a better read on the underlying trend, recorded a
sizeable decline.
Note that most figures in the charts and table are presented on
a rolling-three month basis to smooth out monthly volatility.
Private detached dwelling approvals fell 5.3%mth in
September, unwinding August’s upwardly-revised 5.4%mth
rise (+1.7ppts). Recent resilience has kept the segment
elevated, with approvals remaining above 10,000 and the

annual pace at 9.6%yr. However, the quarterly profile provides
a clearer read on the underlying trend and suggests detached
dwelling approvals are beginning to respond to recent interest
rate increases (see here). Approvals rose just 0.9%qtr in
September vs 3.1%qtr in June and 5.9%qtr in March.
Private unit approvals remained volatile, rising 16.6%mth in
September following 21.7%mth decline in August. Strength
was led by high-rise approvals, while low-mid-rise approvals

recorded a more modest increase. Despite the monthly rise,
the annual pace fell further into contraction at –20.5%yr.
Among the states, NSW recorded the strongest result,
with private approvals rising 35.3%mth led by a surge in
private unit approvals. Queensland also recorded an increase
(+9.3%mth), while WA (–2.9%mth), SA (–3.5%mth) and
Victoria (–29.0%mth) all declined.
In value terms, residential building approvals fell 2.8%mth,
driven by a 3.3%mth decline in new residential approvals,

partially offset by a 0.8%mth rise in renovation activity.
Non-residential approvals once again reversed sharply, rising
45.9%mth, as the data centre-dominated ‘other commercial’
segment recorded an 8-fold increase on a non-seasonally
adjusted basis. The total value of building approvals rose
13.0%mth in September. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis,
the value of building approvals rose 8.3%mth overall, but
declined 3.0%mth when excluding the ‘other commercial’

segment.
September’s result reinforces the importance of looking
through headline volatility in unit approvals. While total
approvals rose in the month, detached dwelling approvals
slowed on a quarterly basis and fell across all states, pointing
to softer underlying momentum. The annual pace of total
approvals also moved into contraction for the first time
since January. Interest rate increases, the sharp downturn
in established housing markets and rising costs are likely to

weigh on further growth, with pessimistic consumer sentiment
an additional headwind (see here).
Detached approvals point to softer momentum

3 November 2026
• Average consumer spend across 
the Westpac-DataX Consumer 
Panel remained resilient, rising 
1.5% in Q3 and 4.6%yr. 
• While some of this reflected a price-
driven surge in fuel spend (+0.3%qtr) 
discretionary categories also posted 
robust gains (contributing 0.9ppts 
to the headline rise) with non-fuel 
essential categories contributing 
positively as well (+0.5ptps).

and buffers. Savings rose 1.3%qtr, led 
by younger households and mortgagors, 
with average savings buffers increasing 
to 22.2 months of mortgage repayments. 
• The detail shows financial pressures 
remain uneven but spending patterns 
appear to be converging across 
consumers with low, medium and high 
buffers. Much of this appears to relate 
to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.
Powered by 
Fuel and non-fuel spending lift

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel  shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25.

Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

Anthropic 发布能实时加⼊视频会议并执⾏任务的 Claude Meetings ，让 Claude 作为参会者
加⼊⾕歌 Google Meet 视频会议，从会议记录员变成实时协作者； AI ⽩板和电⼦表格上
线，发布⽀持团队共同编辑的 Claude Whiteboards 和可由 AI 创建和修改的 Claude
Sheets ；数据分析产品 Claude Data 正式上线，打通企业数据与第三⽅应⽤。微软股价跌
幅扩⼤，⼀度跌超 3% 。
摘要：
Anthropic 在启动 IPO 路演当天密集推出⼀系列企业办公⼯具，覆盖视频会议、电⼦表格、团队
⽩板和企业数据分析等场景。在⾸次公开募股进⼊关键阶段之际，这家公司进⼀步展⽰了
Claude 在基础模型之外的企业应⽤布局，试图向投资者证明其商业化潜⼒不只来⾃ AI 模型本
⾝，也来⾃对企业办公市场的持续渗透。
美东时间 10 ⽇周⼆， Anthropic 发布三项新⼯具：能够实时加⼊视频会议并执⾏任务的 Claude
Meetings 、⽀持团队共同编辑的 Claude Whiteboards ，以及可由 AI 创建和修改的 Claude

Sheets 。此外，原名 Claude Dashboards 的数据分析产品正式更名为 Claude Data 并全⾯推出，
Anthropic 还扩展了与 Adobe 、 Atlassian 、 Box 、 Notion 等应⽤的交互式连接器。
这些新功能试图让 Claude 不再只是回答问题、⽣成⽂本的聊天机器⼈，⽽是能够直接参与会
议、整理任务、制作表格并协同完成⼯作。通过将 AI 嵌⼊企业⽇常⼯作流程， Anthropic 正把竞
争范围从模型性能和 API 价格，进⼀步扩展⾄微软 Office 和⾕歌 Workspace ⻓期占据优势的办公
软件市场。
Anthropic 发布新⼯具后，周⼆美股午盘时段，微软股价跌幅陡然扩⼤，刷新⽇低时⽇内跌超
3% 。
Claude 从会议记录员变成实时协作者，边讨论边执⾏任务
此次发布中最受关注的是 Claude Meetings 。⽤⼾可以将 Claude 作为⼀个可⻅的参会者加⼊⾕歌
Google Meet 视频会议，让它跟随讨论内容，在会议进⾏期间就开始处理相关⼯作，⽽不只是会

后⽣成会议纪要。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

根据 Anthropic 的介绍，参会者可以在讨论中提出需求，例如为团队开发⼀款应⽤，或分析某组
业务数据， Claude 能够调⽤连接的⼯作应⽤获取相关信息，并实时起草⽅案、制定时间表、回
答会议聊天中的问题。会议结束后，会议记录、⾏动事项以及 Claude ⽣成的其他材料都会保存
在 Claude 平台中。
这⼀功能的核⼼变化，是将 AI 介⼊⼯作的时间点从会议结束后提前到讨论过程中。过去，团队通
常需要先开会、整理纪要，再将任务分配给不同成员；现在， Claude 可以在讨论尚未结束时同
步⽣成计划和交付物，帮助团队缩短从讨论到执⾏的间隔。
不过， Claude Meetings ⽬前仍处于测试阶段，⾸先⾯向 Anthropic Team 和 Enterprise 客⼾开
放，初期⽀持 Google Meet ， Anthropic 计划后续扩展⾄其他会议平台。为避免未经授权地进⼊
外部会议， Claude 会在会议聊天中表明⾃⼰的参会⾝份；如果有外部访客加⼊，系统会⾃动让
Claude 退出，除⾮管理员事先授权。

AI ⽩板和电⼦表格上线， Claude 进⼀步切⼊ Office 核⼼场景
除会议⼯具外， Anthropic 还推出 Claude Whiteboards 和 Claude Sheets ，分别瞄准团队头脑⻛
暴和⽇常表格处理两类⾼频办公需求。
Claude Whiteboards 是⼀款⽀持多⼈实时协作的数字⽩板。团队成员可以与 Claude 共同梳理想
法、整理讨论内容， Claude 能够将零散的意⻅归纳成结构化的⼯作空间，并⾃动⽣成包含负责
⼈、⽇期和截⽌时间的时间表及⾏动计划。⽤⼾还可以选中⽩板上的特定区域向 Claude 提问，
讨论完成后将内容进⼀步转换为⽂档或演⽰⽂稿。
这款⽩板⼯具也可以与 Claude Meetings 配合使⽤：当团队在视频会议中讨论项⽬时，⽩板能够
同步形成，会议结束后⽆需再从头整理讨论结果。 Whiteboards ⽬前以测试版形式向付费⽤⼾开
放，⽀持⽹⻚、桌⾯和移动端。
Claude Sheets 则将 AI 直接引⼊电⼦表格。⽤⼾只需描述需求，例如制作营销活动追踪表或招聘

预算表， Claude 就能⽣成包含多个⼯作表、公式、格式和下拉菜单的表格。团队成员可以与
Claude 共同编辑， AI 也能直接修改单元格，并标注每⼀处变动，⽅便⽤⼾检查和追踪。
但 Anthropic 也承认， Sheets ⽬前只具备基础的电⼦表格功能，尚不能替代 Excel 的完整能⼒。⽤
⼾如需更复杂的表格处理，可以将⽂件导出为 .xlsx 或 CSV 格式，或继续使⽤ Claude for Excel 等
⼯具。 Anthropic 产品负责⼈也将其描述为相对基础的产品，并⾮⾯向复杂财务建模等专业场
景。
Whiteboards 和 Sheets 均已在⽹⻚、桌⾯和移动端向付费⽤⼾推出测试版。

### Page 3

Claude Data 正式上线，打通企业数据与第三⽅应⽤
Anthropic 还将此前处于测试阶段的 Claude Dashboards 更名为 Claude Data ，并正式向⽤⼾推
出。这款产品可以连接企业数据仓库和客⼾关系管理系统，让⽤⼾直接⽤⾃然语⾔查询公司数
据，并⽣成仪表盘、书⾯数据报告或带旁⽩的视频。
Claude Data 此次新增的能⼒包括经企业数据团队认证的指标定义、实时分享和版本追踪等。企
业可以为关键指标设定统⼀的查询⼝径，减少不同团队使⽤不同算法⽣成报表的情况；获得授
权的同事则可以查看持续更新的数据结果，⽽不必直接获得底层数据源的访问权限。
与此同时， Anthropic 升级了交互式连接器，⽤⼾可以在 Claude 对话旁边或全屏打开 Adobe 、
Atlassian 、 Box 、 Notion 等第三⽅应⽤，并直接在 Claude 中与这些应⽤协作，⽽不必频繁切换
⼯作窗⼝。 Claude Data 还⽀持连接 Amazon Redshift 、 Google BigQuery 、 ClickHouse 等数据

平台，合作伙伴包括 Amplitude 、 Mixpanel 、 Omni 、 PostHog 和 Sigma 。
这些⼯具背后的产品逻辑，是让 Claude 能够在同⼀⼯作空间中调⽤企业数据和外部应⽤，并在
不同任务之间延续上下⽂。例如，团队可以先在⽩板上讨论项⽬，再将⽅案转成电⼦表格，最
后通过数据⼯具跟踪执⾏结果，⽽不必在每⼀步重新向 AI 解释背景。
从开发者⼯具⾛向企业办公， Anthropic 与微软、⾕歌竞争加剧
这轮产品发布反映出 Anthropic 正在将 Claude 的竞争范围从编程和模型能⼒扩展⾄更⼴泛的知识
⼯作场景。此前， Claude Code 已在软件开发领域建⽴了较强影响⼒；如今， Anthropic 希望将
类似的⼯作⽅式复制到会议、数据分析、表格制作和团队协作中，让⾮技术岗位也能通过⾃然
语⾔直接驱动⼯作流程。
这也意味着 Anthropic 正更直接地进⼊微软和⾕歌的传统优势领域。 Claude Sheets 瞄准 Excel 和
Google Sheets ， Claude Meetings 切⼊视频会议协作， Whiteboards 和 Data 则分别覆盖团队规

划与企业数据分析。 TechCrunch 指出， Anthropic 此番布局也紧随 OpenAI 近期推出的⼀系列办
公功能， AI 公司正竞相将聊天机器⼈扩展为覆盖多种办公任务的综合⼯作平台。
不过， Anthropic 并未试图在所有场景中完全取代现有软件。 Sheets ⽬前仍是基础版本，⽀持导
出⾄ Excel 等⼯具； Whiteboards 也允许⽤⼾将内容继续带⼊ Lucid 、 Miro 等协作平台。与其说
Anthropic 要⽴即替代传统办公软件，不如说它正试图将 Claude 变成连接这些软件、数据和⼯作
流程的 AI ⼊⼝。

⾸次公开募股路演，拟发⾏ 8.25 亿股 A 类普通股，其中约 8.014 亿股由公司发⾏，其余约 2365 万
股来⾃现有股东。发⾏价区间为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元，股票拟以代码 “ANT” 在纳斯达克全球精选市场
上市。
按发⾏价区间计算，此次发⾏总规模约为 577.5 亿⾄ 643.5 亿美元。此前报道称， Anthropic 此次
IPO 计划募资最⾼约 644 亿美元，对应估值约 1.02 万亿美元。最终发⾏价格和上市估值仍有待定
价结果确认。
在 IPO 路 演 期 间 集 中 展 ⽰ 企 业 办 公 产 品 ， 令 这 轮 发 布 具 有 了 技 术 竞 争 之 外 的 商 业 意 义 ：

对投资者⽽⾔，关键问题不仅是模型能否继续保持技术竞争⼒，还在于这些⼯具能否带来更多
企业客⼾、更⾼使⽤频率和可持续的收⼊增⻓。随着 Anthropic 、 OpenAI 以及微软、⾕歌等公司
围绕 AI 办公⼊⼝展开竞争，未来企业软件市场的竞争焦点，或将从单⼀模型的性能和价格，进⼀
步转向谁能更深⼊地嵌⼊企业⼯作流程，并真正承担起从信息处理到任务执⾏的⼯作。

Document 386: NZ labour market review chartpack, Q3 2026 (2026-11-10)

### Page 1

LABOUR MARKET CHARTPACK
4 Nov 2026

### Page 2

Westpac New Zealand Economics
2
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Key points – September quarter 2026
• The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in labour market conditions. The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an
almost five-year run of increases. The unemployment rate is now at its highest level since September 2015.
• There were mixed messages in terms of jobs growth: the Household Labour Force Survey reported a 0.1% fall in employment, while the employer-

focused Quarterly Employment Survey suggested a modest rise. The Monthly Employment Indicator had also pointed to a small rise in jobs.
• The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in the participation rate. These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly large
increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error in the survey rather than a
genuine surge.
• We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message here is one of softness. Our

best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population.
• While the economy has returned to growth over the last year or so, hiring activity remains weak. This may be an ongoing legacy of employment trends in
previous years, when firms were relatively reluctant to lay people off during the slowdown, which then potentially left them overstaffed even as activity
started to pick up again. It’s not clear how long this effect can persist, though.

• Wage growth remains muted. Although cost-of-living pressures have increased on workers, the existing degree of spare capacity in the labour market
doesn’t give them much leverage to negotiate larger pay increases.
• Overall, we judge these results to be weaker than the RBNZ expected, in particular the rise in the unemployment rate. This data supports the views of
those on the policy committee who were less convinced in October about the potential for second-round inflation pressures in the economy.

### Page 3

Westpac New Zealand Economics
3
Employment
• The number of people employed fell by 0.1% in the September quarter, according to the
Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS). Previous quarters were revised down a little,
reflecting downward revisions to the working-age population estimates. Annual employment
growth slowed from 0.9% to 0.7%.
• This was substantially softer than our forecast of a 0.5% rise for the quarter. Our forecast
was based on the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI), a comprehensive measure that is

drawn from income tax data. While there are some differences in coverage for these two
measures, any significant divergence between the two is most likely to reflect sampling error
in the HLFS.
• Meanwhile, the employer-focused Quarterly Employment Survey (QES) recorded a 0.1% rise
in the number of filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. (This survey is of course vulnerable
to sampling error as well.)
• We’re most inclined towards the MEI measure of employment, given the breadth of its

coverage. But our best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not
yet fast enough to absorb the growth in the adult population – hence the further rise in the
unemployment rate.

### Page 4

Westpac New Zealand Economics
4
Labour force participation
• The surprising fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a drop in the participation rate
from 70.6% to 70.4%.
• These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were suggestive of sampling error in the
survey. Any such errors largely wash out when it comes to the unemployment rate, however,
which is why we recommend focusing on this measure.

• Setting aside the usual quarterly volatility, labour force participation remains caught
between two competing forces. One is cyclical weakness, especially among younger people,
who are finding it difficult to break into a “low-fire, low-hire” labour market. The teen
unemployment rate of around 25% is a record high going back to the start of the survey
in 1986.
• The other force is rising participation among the over-60s (who also make up a growing

share of the population). Kiwis have had a growing propensity to work beyond the NZ Super
age, as there is no penalty for doing so. It’s unclear to what extent this can be considered
an increase in the pool of available workers: older workers tend to have low mobility
between jobs, and if they find themselves out of work are more likely to leave the labour
force altogether.

### Page 5

Westpac New Zealand Economics
5
Unemployment and underutilisation
• The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7% in the September quarter. This was the
highest rate since September 2015.
• While there are other indicators of spare capacity in the labour market, we’re not convinced
that there is additional information to draw from them this time.
• The broader underutilisation measure, which includes discouraged jobseekers and part-
time workers wanting more hours, was steady at 13.8% after having risen sharply from

12.9% last quarter. This measure is both higher (by definition) and more variable than the
unemployment rate, but tells a similar story in that it’s around an 11-year high.
• There was a marked 1.6% fall in the number of hours worked (although ‘usual’ hours were
down just 0.4%). However, we suspect this was largely driven by Matariki – a floating holiday
that won’t be captured in the seasonal adjustment.
• This may also account for the 2.5% rise in part-time employment, against a 0.7% fall in

full-time employment. Some workers may have been pushed into the part-time category
(less than 30 hours a week) simply by virtue of only working four days during the
survey week.

### Page 6

Westpac New Zealand Economics
6
Hiring
• Business confidence surveys have shown a lift in hiring intentions in recent months, as firms
have shaken off the initial shock of the Iran conflict and the surge in fuel prices.
• However, those stated intentions aren’t obviously turning into reality yet. Job advertisements
were down about 1% for the September quarter in seasonally adjusted terms, and they
remain well below pre-Covid levels. Job ads will capture ‘churn’ within existing roles as well

as new job creation, but both of these would be associated with a strong labour market.
• With the working-age population growing at the same time, the result was a modest fall in
the vacancy rate, one of the RBNZ’s highest-ranked measures of labour market tightness.
• We estimate that there are around 1.9 people unemployed for every job vacancy, up from
around 1.8 people this time last year.
• Firms have told us that they were reluctant to lay off workers during the economic downturn

in 2023-24, as they were wary of the extreme labour shortages that had developed in the
previous boom years. As a result, they may find themselves still overstaffed even as demand
has started to pick up again, and haven’t needed to return to hiring yet.

### Page 7

Westpac New Zealand Economics
7
Population and migration
• The working-age population grew by around 1.0% over the last year, compared to a peak of
3.3% growth over 2023. Jobs growth (based on the less volatile MEI measure) is still lagging
behind population growth on an annual basis.
• The pool of potential workers is generally becoming older, with the Baby Boomers
increasingly reaching pension age and beyond, and with migrant arrivals tending to be an

older cohort than we saw pre-Covid – more in their 30s and 40s than their 20s.
• Net migration flows have started to pick up from their lows since late 2025. Departures of
New Zealanders have come down from their highs, though they remain above pre-Covid
levels, as the relative appeal of the Australian jobs market has narrowed.
• Foreign arrivals have picked up to some degree, although the growth appears to have been
mostly driven by students rather than those on work visas.

### Page 8

Westpac New Zealand Economics
8
Sectoral and regional trends
• Jobs growth has been relatively strong in government-connected sectors such as healthcare
and education, as well as in local and central government administration.
• Mining and utilities have seen strong growth in percentage terms, but neither of these
sectors are big employers in absolute terms.
• The construction sector is now starting to see a lift in employment, after a steep decline

over 2024-25. Residential building consents have risen strongly over the last year or so,
pointing to a growing pipeline of work for the homebuilding industry.
• Sectors that are connected to international tourism, such as transport and recreational
services, had previously seen strong employment growth, but this appears to have flattened
out in recent months.
• Employment trends have generally been more positive in the South Island than the North,

which likely reflects the greater influence of the agricultural sector – while not a large
employer in itself, farmers have benefited from high export prices and their spending has
flowed into other sectors.

### Page 9

Westpac New Zealand Economics
9
Wages
• The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter. Annual growth
remained around the lowest since 2021.
• While the overall result was in line with our forecast, the details were a little weaker than
we expected, with less contribution from private sector wages (up 0.5%) and a surprisingly
large 0.7% increase in the public sector.
• The distribution of pay rates has returned to pre-Covid trends. Only 44% of roles saw a

pay rise over the last year; of those roles that did see an increase, the average size has
converged on the 2-3% range.
• The LCI is not a measure of what workers actually receive – it reflects the cost to businesses
for a given unit of labour – and hence shouldn’t be compared to the inflation rate. A more
suitable measure is the analytical unadjusted LCI, which includes pay rises that are related
to productivity gains. This measure slowed from 3.1% to 2.9% on an annual basis, the lowest

since March 2021.
• There was a marked pickup in the proportion of firms reporting cost-of-living adjustments as
the main reason for pay increases. However, the size of those increases varied greatly, with
many of them below the rate of CPI inflation.
• This seems to reflect a shift in the justification for pay rises, rather than an overall increase in
wage pressures – firms are more likely to cite cost-of-living adjustments because they’re less
likely to cite demand-side pressures.

### Page 10

Westpac New Zealand Economics
10
International comparisons
• New Zealand’s unemployment rate is the 18th lowest in the OECD (out of 38 countries). This
ranking has been broadly unchanged over the last year or so.
• Notably, New Zealand’s unemployment rate is substantially above Australia’s, whereas
historically it has been lower on average. This shift in relative job opportunities has been a
key attractor for New Zealanders over the last couple of years – as it was in the early 2010s

when the Australian economy was benefiting from a boom in mining construction.
• However, that gap between unemployment rates is no longer widening, with the Australian
jobs market starting to soften this year as the RBA has raised interest rates to rein in inflation
(which was already running too high before the Iran conflict and the surge in oil prices). We
expect Australia’s unemployment to rise further over the year ahead.
• New Zealand has the 7th highest employment rate in the OECD (down from 6th last quarter).

We tend to have high rates of labour force participation for 15-64 year olds, and particularly
high participation for the over-65s, as the lack of means testing for NZ Superannuation
means there is no financial disincentive to working beyond pension age.

Document 450: Westpac NZ Retail Spending Pulse, October 2026 (2026-10-01)

RETAIL

### Page 2

Westpac New Zealand Economics
2
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5668 | +64 21 710 852 | satish.ranchhod@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Discretionary spending flat as high fuel
prices bite
• Per-person spending on Westpac issued credit and debit cards was up 1.2% in October and is 7.8%
higher than this time last year.
October spending.
• October’s rise in spending was mainly related to the sharp increase in petrol prices, with the average

price for 91-unleaded around the country climbing to a record high of over $3.60/ltr. That saw
spending on fuel rise 14% over the month.
• Those higher fuel prices, along with other cost of living pressures, have put the brakes on other
purchases. Spending in discretionary areas was down 0.4% in October and has essentially been
tracking sideways since May. There’s been notable softness in spending on travel and household
furnishings. However, spending in restaurants has been holding up.

Annual growth.
• While spending has certainly been constrained by the rise in living costs since the start of this year,
spending levels are still well above those we saw in 2025. And that’s not just because of high fuel
prices, with spending in discretionary areas also up compared to this time last year.
• That’s been supported by factors such as the drop in borrowing costs over the past year and
strength in export earnings. The boost from those factors is helping to balance the impacts of

factors such as softness in the labour market and high fuel prices. However, as we highlight over the
page, the strength in spending has been uneven across the country.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
3
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5668 | +64 21 710 852 | satish.ranchhod@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Regional breakdown
• As has been the case for some time, spending growth has been strongest in Southland. Other parts
of the country with strong rural backbones like Canterbury and Northland have also continued to
see solid gains.
• Spending growth remains more modest in Auckland. However, we expect that spending in Auckland

will take a step higher next month, with hospitality spending in particular likely to be boosted by
events like the Indian cricket tour, Diwali and Auckland Marathon.
• In a notable move, Wellington has shifted up the ranks and now sits in the middle of the pack.
Wellingtonians are no doubt holding their breath ahead of Saturday’s tight election and many will be
stocking up for the traditional election night barbecue and watch party.*

企業景況感（日銀短観）
日銀短観9月調査の業況判断DIは、全産業で＋22ptと6月調査(＋19pt)から改善した。製造業では、非鉄金
属、生産用機械、電気機械、自動車等が改善し、全体でも改善となる一方、非製造業では建設、小売、情報
通信などが悪化し、全体としては小幅悪化したものの、引き続き高い水準を維持している。先行き（12月）につ
いては、製造業、非製造業とも悪化が見込まれている。

Document 543: 中科星云发布“星云星座计划”，拟建“天地对等”太空算力网

Source PDF: all report pdf/中科星云发布“星云星座计划”，拟建“天地对等”太空算力网.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

11 ⽉ 9 ⽇， 2026 太空云产业⽣态⼤会暨中科星云成⽴⼤会在合肥举⾏。中科星云太空智能科技
（合肥）有限公司（以下简称 " 中科星云 " ）在会上宣布投⼊运营，认缴注册资本 15 亿元。⼯商登
记信息显⽰，该公司已于 11 ⽉ 2 ⽇完成设⽴登记。
作为 “ 太空云 ” 专项的重要产业化主体，中科星云将依托中国科学院空天信息创新研究院（以下简
称 " 空天院 " ）的技术积累，整合国产算⼒芯⽚、服务器、卫星测运控、星座建设及空天信息服务
等产业资源，布局太空算⼒基础设施，探索构建天地⼀体化的太空智能服务体系。
公司由中科星图、中科曙光、海光信息、星图测控、曙光数创、中科卫星 6 家产业主体，联合中
国科学院旗下国科发展、央企诚通科创，安徽省属皖能资本、华安嘉业及省科技厅牵头主管的
⺟基⾦，以及合肥产投、合肥⾼新创投等市、区两级投资平台，共计 16 家发起主体共同设⽴。
其背后，是空天院⻓期以来在空天信息领域的科研积累。此前，空天院联合国内多家科研优势
单位，构建起 " 太空云 " 的概念体系、技术体制与基础平台，⽬标是把遥感、通信、算⼒三类卫星

通过技术⼿段整合为统⼀调度的天基信息服务系统，推动卫星从单⼀功能载体向协同⼯作的 " 智
能星云 " 演进。
⼤会期间，相关各⽅就 " 太空云 " 专项知识产权达成授权意向，会上发布 " 星云星座计划 " 。
与传统卫星主要承担数据采集、传输任务不同，太空算⼒的核⼼在于将计算能⼒部署到卫星及
空间⽹络之中，使卫星具备在轨处理数据、协同计算和智能服务的能⼒。
6 家产业发起主体中，海光信息提供国产⾼端算⼒芯⽚，中科曙光提供服务器整机与智算基础设
施，星图测控提供卫星测运控能⼒，曙光数创提供算⼒基础设施热管理⽀撑，中科卫星提供感
算星座建设和运营，中科星图贯通空天数据与信息服务，覆盖了天基信息服务从芯⽚到应⽤的
完整领域。
在此基础上，中科星云将坚持全栈国产化⾃主技术路线，重点布局太空算⼒芯⽚、太空计算
机、星间⾼速互联及天地资源调度平台等关键领域，探索形成⾃主可控的技术体系与标准。
在技术发展⽅向上，中科星云提出建设和运营 " 天地对等 " 的太空算⼒⽹。
所谓 " 天地对等 " ，并不是简单追求太空算⼒规模与地⾯算⼒完全相同，⽽是希望太空算⼒能够在

规模、价格和应⽤层⾯与地⾯算⼒实现对等协同，使太空成为与地⾯算⼒⽹络地位同等、能⼒
互补、统⼀调度的信息⽹络节点。
为实现这⼀⽬标，公司提出沿着 " 感算、通算、超算 " 三个阶段逐步推进。
第⼀阶段感算融合，卫星在采集数据的同时完成处理，实现 " 边采边算、即拍即算 " 。第⼆阶段通
算融合，依托星间⾼速互联与在轨组⽹计算，⽀撑巨型遥感星座协同运⾏。第三阶段超算协
同，汇聚在轨算⼒构建轨道数据中⼼，形成 " 通感算⽤ " ⼀体化、天地统⼀调度的太空算⼒⽹络。
从单颗卫星的感知计算，到多颗卫星之间的协同组⽹，再到⼤规模在轨算⼒资源的统⼀调度，
这⼀路径体现了太空算⼒从单点能⼒建设⾛向系统级基础设施建设的技术演进⽅向。
除了技术和基础设施建设，中科星云也将着⼒构建开放协同的产业运营体系。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

图⽚
公司提出依托三⼤核⼼⽣态推动产业发展。⼀是深度融⼊空天院牵头建设的 " 太空云 " ⽣态，联合
科研机构、航天企业及⾼校，推动感知、通信、计算、智能⼀体化的天基信息服务体系建设。
⼆是计划借助中科曙光与海光信息的运营⽣态，探索将地⾯智算基础设施和国产算⼒芯⽚的运
营能⼒向太空延伸，建⽴天地⼀体的算⼒运营体系。三是联合产业链上下游企业，共同建设和
运营星云太空基础设施，形成开放、协同、可持续的产业发展模式。
中科星云选择落地合肥，也与当地近年来在商业航天及硬科技领域的持续布局密切相关。
在新能源汽⻋、集成电路、新型显⽰等领域形成产业集群后，合肥正进⼀步向空天信息和商业
航天领域延伸，推动产业资源从卫星数据应⽤向卫星制造、⼤规模卫星运营等更上游环节拓
展。
⽬前，合肥已形成涵盖卫星制造、卫星运营、卫星数据应⽤等环节的产业基础。

Source PDF: all report pdf/乌美俄三方首日会谈结束，泽连斯基称“具有建设性”，乌方再提俄乌直接谈判.pdf

### Page 1

各⽅重点讨论战俘释放、安全保障机制及降级举措；乌⽅提交⿊海、能源领域专项停⽕⽅
案，并呼吁泽连斯基与普京直接会⾯以加速终战。乌⽅外⻓瑟⽐加认为，乌俄领导⼈直接
会⾯有望加速冲突结束。三⽅定于次⽇继续谈判。
摘要：
乌外⻓：乌俄领导⼈直接会⾯有望加速冲突结束
当地时间 10 ⽇，乌克兰外⻓瑟⽐加表⽰，在阿联酋阿布扎⽐举⾏的乌美俄三⽅谈判中，乌⽅提
出多项核⼼诉求：缓和局势、落实停⽕、建⽴安全保障机制，以及推动乌总统泽连斯基与俄总
统普京举⾏会晤。
瑟⽐加认为，乌俄领导⼈直接会⾯有望加速冲突结束。乌⽅同时提交⿊海、能源领域专项停⽕
⽅案，⽬前等待俄⽅回应。
当地时间 10 ⽇中午，俄罗斯、美国和乌克兰新⼀轮三⽅会谈在阿联酋阿布扎⽐启动，探讨推动
乌克兰危机政治解决的途径。这也是今年 2 ⽉谈判陷⼊停滞以来，三⽅时隔 9 个⽉重启⾯对⾯磋
商。
当地时间 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇，乌克兰总统泽连斯基在社交媒体发⽂称，乌克兰、美国和俄罗斯代表团在
阿联酋阿布扎⽐举⾏的⾸⽇三⽅会谈富有建设性，磋商内容充实。各⽅重点讨论战俘释放、⼈

道主义举措、乌克兰⻓期安全保障机制以及缓和局势等议题。泽连斯基已向乌谈判团队下达后
续任务，要求继续推进三⽅会谈以及同欧美代表的外交磋商。
本⽂来源：央视新闻
泽连斯基：乌美俄三⽅⾸⽇会谈具有建设性
泽连斯基在听取代表团汇报后介绍，本次谈判分为两个阶段：乌⽅先同欧美伙伴开展协调磋
商，随后举⾏乌美俄三⽅会谈。他强调，战俘释放是乌⽅优先事项。
在安全保障议题上，各⽅细化探讨对乌安全保障的具体内容，以及保障机制⻓期有效运⾏所需
的基础条件。泽连斯基表⽰，乌克兰需要建⽴可靠有效的安全保障机制，防范冲突再起。会谈
同时谈及缓和紧张局势的相关举措。
泽连斯基还表⽰，已明确代表团下⼀步⼯作安排。按计划，乌美俄三⽅将于次⽇继续会谈；乌
⽅还将在未来数⽇内持续同美国特使及欧洲代表开展磋商。他称，乌⽅核⼼⽬标是推动和平进
程取得实质进展，将以务实、认真、坦诚的态度参与后续谈判。
泽连斯基：欧洲应参与旨在结束俄乌冲突的谈判
乌克兰总统泽连斯基 10 ⽇接受采访时说，欧洲应参与旨在结束俄乌冲突的谈判，以及由美国和

欧洲向乌克兰提供安全保障的讨论。
泽连斯基当天访问丹⻨，同丹⻨⾸相弗雷泽⾥克森举⾏会谈。根据丹⻨政府公布的安排，泽连
斯基还将同北欧国家领导⼈讨论对乌军事援助和欧洲安全。
本⽂来源：央视新闻

### Page 1

2026 年 11 月 9 日 • 產業概況更新

光通訊產業

光模組數量及規格升級，陶瓷元件需求提升且高導熱與穩定訊號為特點

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。

陳玫芬  合格證券投資分析人員  & 
CFP 
Lisa.mf.chen@yuanta.com 
江誼蓁 
yizhen0808@yuanta.com

元大觀點 
⧫  
光收發模組 400G 以上估 2026/2027 年增 80%/43%至 1 億/1.45 億個；
且規格由 400G 至 800G 及 1.6T 持續升級。NPO/CPO 亦需插拔模組。 
⧫  
陶瓷基板的特性為高導熱與高絕緣，熱膨脹係數接近光晶片，適用於高
頻且低信號損耗。市場上主要陶瓷基板元件供應商以日系為主。 
⧫  
光晶片數量及傳輸速率持續升級對光晶片的聯亞、全新、環宇，及石英
元件的晶技具正面助益；璦司柏陶瓷基板亦同步升級且需求增加。 
光收發模組的規格及數量提升，NPO/CPO 亦需搭插拔式模組 
AI 資料中心需求使光收發模組 (Optical Transceiver) 大幅增長，插拔式光收
發模組具維修便利優勢，發展多年但仍非標準品； 400G 以上光收發模組估
計 2026 年增 80%至 1 億個，2027 年增 43%至 1.45 億個；2026 年以 400G
為主流，但 2027-2029 年規格以 800G 為主流，2026/2027/2028 年的 1.6T
占比為 15%/38%/42%，升級至 1.6T 會採更高階內部零件。 NPO/CPO 於
2026/2027/2028 年估計為 40 萬/320 萬/2,200 萬個，年增幅高但數量遠低於
光收發模組數量；且其 亦需搭配插拔式的模組即 ELSFP ( 外接雷射光源模
組)。 
陶瓷基板的光通訊應用於熱管理及訊號穩定功能，日益重要 
陶瓷基板的特性為高導熱與高絕緣，其熱膨脹係數 (CTE)接近光晶片，適用
於高頻且低信號損耗。光收發模組採用的陶瓷基板元件包含 Interposer(中介
層)、TEC(致冷器)、Submount(次載板)；光收發模組走向 800G 以上長距離，
則陶瓷基板幾乎成為標配 。市場上主要陶瓷基板元件供應商以日系為主，尤
其 Interposer 及 Submount 的領導廠商 Kyocera(京瓷)的市占率均過半數，
而 TEC 則由日系與美系廠商寡占。 
建議關注光通訊相關的台廠，如璦司柏、全新、聯亞、環宇等 
全球 AI 需要頻寬上升，光收發模組訊號穩定性與發熱抑制成為關鍵，光晶
片數量及傳輸速率持續升級對光晶片的聯亞、全新、環宇，及石英元件的晶
技均具正面助益。光晶片的温度控制相當重要，波長飄移則無法穩定傳輸訊
號，且高温對光晶片壽命具絕對影響；璦司柏主要陶瓷基板產品包含 (A) 
Ceramic interposer (B) TEC( 致冷器) (C) Submount ，陶瓷基板特性適合高
頻元件導熱，且以薄膜積體電路技術從事微細線路及穿孔之設計製造與量產
能力為公司競爭力來源，陶瓷基板事業營收比重 2025 年為 51%，成長率為
91%為最重要成長動能。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：光通訊 2026 年 11 月 9 日  第2頁，共12頁   
產業分析  
光收發模組明顯成長且 800G 及 1.6T 零件更高階；NPO/CPO 數量低但高成長 
AI 對高速網路傳輸速率的需求持續攀升，光收發模組 (Optical Transceiver) 扮演關鍵角色；光收發
模組於 400G 以下且市場穩定；然而 400G 以上估年增 2026/2027 年為 80%/43%至 1 億/1.45 億個；
2026 年主流為 400G，2027 及 2028 年主流為 800G 且未來升級至 1.6T 以上則採更高階零組件，
如 Interposer、TEC 及 Submount 等電子級精密陶瓷。NPO/CPO 仍為初始階段，2026/2027/2028
年估 40 萬/320 萬/2,200 萬個：2027/2028 年增 680%/595%，惟數量遠低於光收發模組；CPO 將
OE 與交換機的 ASIC 共同封裝，降低傳輸功耗至 1/3；NPO 僅將 OE 與 ASIC 放置在交換機內同一
PCB 基板上，降低交換機傳輸功耗至 50%；二者均需插拔式 ELSFP 模組。 
圖 1：光收發模組(Optical Transceiver)市場預估數量成長；CPO/NPO 市場仍小但成長性高

插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺，陶瓷元件供給亦有限 
插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺，陶瓷元件(Interposor、TEC、Submount)供給
亦有限；PCB 在高階光模組需 mSAP 製程具門檻， DSP 由國際少數廠商寡占使供需相當緊俏，內
部的發射及接收端光元件晶粒以國際大廠為主，陶瓷元件以日系大廠為主，均勻擴廠因應需求。
